# code-law:guard-form

Declared by [ADR 0382](../../../docs/adr/0382-the-derived-valve-rule-is-the-candidate-and-a-guard-holds-both-shapes.md), build brief H rung H1. This file reports **met or not met** against
the bars as that record
words them. Every line in it is a standing, or a count of the cases that were checked. Nothing
in it measures how well anything did, and nothing in it may be restated as if it did.

The law below was read out of the pinned ICSSim source at `bcfd1844d3f063850c69f2643f6a052bac03d8ec`. The source was read
as text and parsed into a syntax tree. It was not imported, executed or simulated, and
`data/phos-icssim-dev/roles.toml` was not read.
<!-- evidence-ack: bcfd1844d3f063850c69f2643f6a052bac03d8ec — the pinned ICSSIM checkout's head under data/, not a commit in this repository -->

## The bars

| # | Bar | Standing | What was found |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | literal agreement, quoted from the source node | met | close 0.0 written `0` from src/PLC1.py:18; open 1.0 written `1` from src/PLC1.py:20; release_mode 3.0 written `3` from src/Configs.py:49; belt_past_filler 1.0 written `1.0` from src/PLC1.py:26 |
| 2 | comparison agreement on both sides | met | above the maximum closes, below the minimum opens, the band is silence; full or past the filler closes, every other reading opens |
| 3 | guard comparison is on the guard's form | met | derived form is exclusion of {1.0, 2.0}; hand form is equality at 3.0; forms differ |
| 4 | forced actuator writes at modes 1 and 2 are a stated fact | met | mode 1.0 writes 0.0 (`0` at src/ics_sim/Device.py:409); mode 2.0 writes 1.0 (`1` at src/ics_sim/Device.py:412) |
| 5 | every disagreement on the extended enumeration is listed, with a certificate | met | 8820 cases on modes [0.0, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 3.3, 4.0, 100.0]; 4320 disagreements listed, each with a certificate |
| 6 | certificate shape | met | 154 certificates checked, each naming its rows and its false comparison |
| 7 | the tree stays green | not met | not computed here: the suite, mypy --strict src, ruff and an empty diff on declared_law.py are reported by the session that runs them |

**Overall: not met.** A bar that is not met is a refusal of this
step as ADR 0382 words it. `controller_valve` is not edited, and its literals do not move. The
derived rule stays a candidate.

## The rules the derivation read

**input valve** - writes `tank_input_valve_status`, guarded on `tank_input_valve_mode`.
1. `tank_level_value` > `tank_level_max` → writes 0.0 (line 17)
2. `tank_level_value` < `tank_level_min` → writes 1.0 (line 19)
Otherwise: nothing is required, which is silence.

**output valve** - writes `tank_output_valve_status`, guarded on `tank_output_valve_mode`.
1. `bottle_level_value` > `bottle_level_max` or `bottle_distance_to_filler_value` > `1.0` → writes 0.0 (line 26)
Otherwise: writes 1.0.

## The guard the derivation read

`src/ics_sim/Device.py`:404 is an exclusion of modes
1.0, 2.0. The hand guard is an equality at the
release literal. The two forms differ. That comparison is on the form, not on a hand-picked list
of modes.
<!-- evidence-ack: src/ics_sim/Device.py — AlirezaDehlaghi/ICSSIM's own layout in the pinned checkout under data/, not a path in this repository -->

silence is not listed as agreement on a decision.
